# Graph Report - MythosMUD  (2026-08-17)

## Corpus Check
- 3314 files · ~2,837,578 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 53782 nodes · 101099 edges · 2611 communities (1829 shown, 782 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 93% EXTRACTED · 7% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 7486 edges (avg confidence: 0.54)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `a80796e2`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
